## Atlantic Fishery Regulations, 1985 {#atlantic-fishery-regulations-1985 .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/86-21
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Registration 1985-12-19

Atlantic Fishery Regulations, 1985

P.C. 1985-3662 1985-12-19
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, pursuant to sections 8,
34[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_1e){#fn_1e-ID0E2FAA},
34.3[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*^](#fn_2e){#fn_2e-ID0EZFAA},
51[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*^](#fn_3e){#fn_3e-ID0EXFAA} and
69[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*\*^](#fn_4e){#fn_4e-ID0EVFAA} of the
[Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14), is pleased hereby to revoke the
Atlantic Coast Marine Plant Regulations, C.R.C., c. 805; the Atlantic
Crab Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 806; the Atlantic Fishery
Regulations, C.R.C., c. 807; the Atlantic Fishing Registration and
Licensing Regulations, C.R.C., c. 808; the Atlantic Herring Fishery
Regulations made by P.C. 1983-23 of 13 January, 1983[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*\*\*^](#fn_5e){#fn_5e-ID0EHFAA}; the Fishing Gear
Marking Regulations, C.R.C., c. 813; and the Lobster Fishery
Regulations, C.R.C., c. 817, and to make the annexed Regulations
respecting the management and allocation of fishery resources on the
Atlantic coast of Canada, in substitution therefor, effective January 8,
1986.

  [[Capelin Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      Fishing Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule IV; ([zone de
      pêche du capelan]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class Sc-4 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-4 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,

  [[Class Sc-5 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-5 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/89-584, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Class Sc-6 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-6 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,

  [[Class Sc-7 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-7 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,

  [[Class Sc-8 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-8 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/89-584, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Class Sc-9 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class Sc-9 vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/89-584, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Class T vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class T vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/89-584, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Class U vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class U vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/89-584, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Class V vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class V vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/89-584, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cod-end]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cod-end]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-like extension that is
      attached to the after end of the belly of an otter trawl and is
      used to retain the catch; ([cul de chalut]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Convention on Future
      Multilateral Cooperation in the Northwest Atlantic Fisheries that
      was ratified by Canada on November 30, 1978 and came into force on
      January 1, 1979; ([Convention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Convention Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Convention Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of the
      Northwest Atlantic Ocean north of 35°00′ north latitude and west
      of a line extending due north from 35°00′ north latitude and
      42°00′ west longitude to 59°00′ north latitude, thence due west to
      44°00′ west longitude, and thence due north to the coast of
      Greenland, and the waters of the Gulf of St. Lawrence, Davis
      Strait and Baffin Bay south of 78°10′ north latitude; ([zone de la
      Convention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Crab Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Crab Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Crab Fishing
      Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XI; ([zone de pêche du
      crabe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a calendar
      day; ([jour]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Department of
      Fisheries and Oceans; ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-like net that is fixed
      to a frame to which a handle is attached and is used to catch fish
      without enmeshing them; ([épuisette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a division described in
      Schedule III; ([division]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[drift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[drift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a gill net that drifts in
      the water whether or not it is attached to a vessel and includes a
      gill net that is not anchored at both ends; ([filet
      dérivant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fishing trip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fishing trip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a voyage that commences
      at the time a fishing vessel leaves a port to engage in fishing
      and terminates at the time fish caught during that period are
      offloaded; ([expédition de pêche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fixed gear]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fixed gear]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing gear other than
      mobile gear, angling gear, a drift net, a crab trap, a lobster
      trap or a dip net; ([engin fixe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-61, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net by which fish are
      caught by being enmeshed but that does not enclose an area of
      water; ([filet maillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[groundfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[groundfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any species of fish set
      out in Part II of Schedule I; ([poisson de fond]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Herring Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Herring Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Herring
      Fishing Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule VI; ([zone de
      pêche du hareng]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hoop net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hoop net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a box-shaped or
      funnel-shaped apparatus that is constructed of netting or wire
      mesh fastened to a hoop or frame and that is used to catch fish
      without enmeshing them, and includes any fishing gear commonly
      referred to as a hand bow, hand trap or hand
      pot; ([verveux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incidental catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incidental catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/94-292, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a herring, a mackerel or a salmon, means the
        distance measured in a straight line from the tip of the nose to
        the fork of the tail,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a swordfish, means the distance measured in a
        straight line from the tip of the lower jaw to the fork of the
        tail,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a clam, mussel or oyster, means the distance
        measured in a straight line through the longest part of the
        shell,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a lobster, means the distance from the rear of
        either eye socket to the rear end of the carapace, measured
        along a straight line parallel to the centre line of the
        carapace of the lobster, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a groundfish, means the distance measured in a
        straight line from the tip of the nose or, if the head has been
        removed, from the foremost point of the fish to the fork of the
        tail or, if the tail has been removed, to the end of the
        fish; ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence authorizing a
      person to fish for the species of fish specified in the licence,
      to harvest the species of marine plants specified in the licence
      or to transport fish as specified in the
      licence; ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Lobster Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Lobster Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Lobster
      Fishing Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XIII; ([zone
      de pêche du homard]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line that has a series of
      fish hooks attached and is used to catch fish but does not include
      angling gear or hand-lines; ([palangre]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Mackerel Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Mackerel Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Mackerel
      Fishing Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule IX; ([zone de
      pêche du maquereau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of netting

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a crab trap, the distance measured in a
        straight line inside the knots forming any one side of a single
        mesh,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in an otter trawl, the average size of any 20
        meshes that are measured by inserting into the meshes a flat
        wedge-shaped gauge having a taper of 2 cm in 8 cm and a
        thickness of 2.3 mm with a weight of 5 kg attached, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total length of the
        twine measured along two contiguous sides of a single mesh,
        including the distance across the knot joining those sides but
        not including any other knots; ([maillage]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[mobile gear]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mobile gear]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means otter trawls, purse
      seines and scallop drags; ([engin mobile]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[monofilament]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[monofilament]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single continuous
      filament made from polyethylene, polyvinylidene chloride or any
      similar type of synthetic material that has not been braided or
      woven; ([monofilament]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[observer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[observer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/93-61, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[otter trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[otter trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      [[otter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or other trawl of a similar nature
      and includes a [[Danish seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, a
      [[Scottish seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and a midwater
      trawl; ([chalut à panneaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[overall length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[overall length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in relation to a vessel,
      means the horizontal distance measured between perpendicular lines
      drawn at the extreme ends of the outside of the main hull of the
      vessel; ([longueur hors tout]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial or territorial fisher's certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial or territorial fisher's
      certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Fishery (General)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53); [(certificat provincial
      ou territorial de pêcheur)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}

  [[recreational fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recreational fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing solely
      for pleasure or fishing where the fish caught are solely for
      personal use; ([pêche récréative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-61, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[registered]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means registered with the
      Department pursuant to section
      17.1; ([enregistré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Salmon Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Salmon Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Salmon Fishing
      Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XXI; ([zone de pêche
      du saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[scallop drag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scallop drag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of scallop
      fishing gear towed in the sea by a vessel and that consists of a
      bag-like net of steel meshes and twine meshes, the closed end of
      the net having attached to it a dumping bar and the open end of
      the net being attached to a steel rectangular frame, and includes
      any other similar fishing gear; ([drague à
      pétoncles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Scallop Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Scallop Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Scallop
      Fishing Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XV; ([zone de
      pêche du pétoncle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that hangs vertically in
      the water with floats attached at the top of the net and weights
      attached at the bottom, the ends of which are brought together or
      hauled ashore, and includes a bar seine, beach seine, drag seine
      or other similar type of net, but does not include a purse
      seine; ([seine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[shell height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shell height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a scallop,
      means the distance from the outer edge of the shell at the
      midpoint of the hinge to the farthest point on the outer edge of
      the shell opposite to the hinge, measured in a straight
      line; ([hauteur de la coquille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Shrimp Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Shrimp Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Shrimp Fishing
      Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XVII; ([zone de pêche
      de la crevette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[square mesh]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[square mesh]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/94-60, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Squid Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Squid Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Squid Fishing
      Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule XIX; ([zone de pêche
      du calmar]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Subarea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Subarea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Subarea described in
      Schedule III; ([sous-zone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Subdivision described
      in Schedule III; ([sous-division]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that is set so as to
      enclose an area of water into which fish are guided through an
      opening or openings by one or more leaders; ([filet-piège or
      trappe en filet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[weir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[weir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an apparatus that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is constructed of stakes attached to the seabed
        and held together with one or more sets of ribands,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is surrounded by brush, twine or wire netting,
        and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consists of one or more holding compartments or
        pounds into which fish are guided by one or more
        leaders; ([bordigue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a clam or crab,
      means the distance measured in a straight line through the
      greatest breadth of the shell. ([largeur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a species or
  group of species of fish or marine plants by its common name as set
  out in an item of Schedule I shall be construed as a reference to the
  species or group of species whose scientific name is set out in that
  item.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to the weight of
  fish shall be construed as a reference to the weight of the fish in a
  completely unprocessed state.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/91-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5) and (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/93-61, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a vessel class
  means a vessel class identified in a licence or vessel registration
  card as one of vessel classes A1 to A3000, B1 to B220, C1 to C6000, D1
  to D220, E to S, Sc-1 to Sc-9 and T to Z.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations, all geographical coordinates
  (latitude and longitude) are expressed in the North American Datum
  1927 (NAD 27) geodetic reference system.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations, the lines connecting points or
  coordinates are rhumb lines, unless stated otherwise.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891753 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) and (3), these Regulations apply to the management
  and control of fishing for the species of fish that are set out in
  Schedule I and that originate from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Convention Area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tidal waters of the Provinces of New Brunswick,
    Newfoundland and Labrador, Nova Scotia, Prince Edward Island and
    Quebec;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the waters of Ungava Bay; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the waters of Hudson Strait lying east of 70°00′
    west longitude.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where no species of fish is referred to in a
  provision of Part IV or XI, that provision applies in respect of any
  species of fish, whether or not that species is set out in Schedule I.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph (3)(c), these Regulations
  apply to the management and control of the harvesting of marine plants
  in the Atlantic coastal waters of Canada.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a foreign fishing vessel or a person fishing on
    board such a vessel, except where the vessel is used for
    recreational fishing in Canadian fisheries waters;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fishing for anadromous or catadromous fish in tidal
    waters of Quebec as defined in the Quebec Fishery Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} manual harvesting of dulse or harvesting of other
    marine plants that have become detached from the bottom;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} angling for salmon; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any cultivated Atlantic salmon that are found in or
    taken from an aquaculture facility or any cultivated clams, mussels,
    oysters or scallops, of any species, that are so found or taken.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph 49.01(c) and subsections 49.3(2)
  and 91(1), the close times set out in these Regulations do not apply
  with respect to recreational fishing in accordance with subsection
  15(1) or (2).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Sections 13 to 14, 17, 17.1, 20 to 22, 39 to 45 and
  46 to 50, subsection 51.3(1) and sections 51.4, 52, 54, 57, 61, 61.1,
  63, 66, 68, 69, 70.1 to 72, 74, 77, 78, 80, 82, 83, 87, 90, 91, 99,
  106, 106.1 and 108 to 115.1 do not apply with respect to fishing and
  related activities carried out under the authority of a licence issued
  under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332) and sections 18 and 19 do
  not apply to the issuance of licences under those Regulations.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (5), only sections 51, 53, 54, 59,
  61, 61.1, 68, 76, 78 to 80 and 86 and Part X apply in respect of Inuit
  domestic fishing, being the exercise by a person of the right to
  harvest any species or stock of fish for food, social and ceremonial
  purposes in accordance with the Agreement defined in section 2 of the
  [Labrador Inuit Land Claims Agreement Act](/eng/acts/L-4.3).

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[[\[Repealed, SOR/93-61, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891773 .Part}

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Registration of Persons and Vessels and Licensing of Persons]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891774 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/91-498, s. 3

\]
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891777 .Subheading}

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fisherman's registration card,
a vessel registration card or a licence.

### [Registration Cards, Licences and Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891779 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2020-255, s. 2

\]
:::

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 15 and subsection
  51.1(2), no person shall use a vessel, and no owner of a vessel shall
  permit another person to use the vessel, in fishing for any species of
  fish referred to in these Regulations unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vessel registration card has been issued in
    respect of the vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of the vessel to fish for that species of
    fish is authorized by a licence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the person who is using
    the vessel is named in the licence referred to in paragraph (b).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a licence is issued authorizing the use of a
  vessel to fish for a species of fish and an operator is not named in
  the licence, any registered fisher or any person holding a provincial
  or territorial fisher's certificate may operate that vessel to fish
  for that species.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-468, s. 5(F)
- SOR/93-61, s. 4
- [SOR/2020-255, s. 3](#1274728-1276723){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1274728-1276723.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall use a vessel, and no owner of a vessel shall permit another
  person to use that vessel, to transport any species of fresh fish
  referred to in these Regulations unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vessel registration card has been issued in
    respect of the vessel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of that vessel to transport that species is
    authorized by a licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply where the vessel used
  in transporting fresh fish is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel that was engaged in the catching of that
    fish; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vessel designated under the authority of a
    licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-498, s. 4
- SOR/93-337, s. 1
- SOR/2017-58, s. 12(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 13.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[13.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.2 .sectionLabel}** No person who is
fishing under the authority of a licence that does not specify the use
of a specific vessel shall permit a vessel to be used to transport any
species of fresh fish caught under the authority of that licence unless
the use of that vessel to transport that species is authorized by a
licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-498, s. 4
:::

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4) and
  section 15, no person shall fish for any species of fish set out in
  Schedule I unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person holds a fisher's registration card or a
    provincial or territorial fisher's certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is authorized under subsection (2) to
    fish for that species.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a person is authorized to
  fish for a species of fish if that person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence for that species;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on board a vessel and is named as the operator of
    that vessel in a licence that authorizes the use of that vessel to
    fish for that species;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} accompanying a person referred to in paragraph (a)
    or (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} on board a vessel the owner of which is the holder
    of a licence that authorizes the use of that vessel in fishing for
    that species and an operator is not named in the licence; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} authorized in accordance with subsection 23(2) of
    the [Fishery (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 2(b) to (d) do not apply to a person
  engaged in recreational fishing.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who is less than 16 years of age may engage
  in fishing without being registered or without holding a provincial or
  territorial fisher's certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 54(E)
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 2](#1275010-1275294){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275010-1275294.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-255, s. 4](#1274730-1276725){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1274730-1276725.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20201123.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of a licence for recreational fishing may fish for a species
  specified in that licence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without being registered or without holding a
    provincial or territorial fisher's certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from a vessel that is not registered.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may, without being registered, without
  holding a provincial or territorial fisher's certificate or without
  being licensed, and with a vessel that is not registered, engage in
  recreational fishing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for groundfish with a hand-line or by angling;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for mackerel with a hand-line or by angling;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for capelin with any fishing gear other than trap
    nets, seines or mobile gear; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for squid with any fishing gear other than trap
    nets or mobile gear.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A person less than 16 years of age who does not
  hold a licence may engage in recreational fishing when accompanied by
  the holder of a recreational fishing licence if the fishing is done in
  accordance with the conditions of the licence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is designated under paragraph 4(2)(a) or
  subsection 4(3) of the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332) to fish under the authority
  of a licence issued under subsection 4(1) of those Regulations may
  engage in fishing in accordance with the conditions of that licence

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without being registered or without holding a
    provincial or territorial fisher's certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from a vessel that is not registered.

::: HistoricalNote

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** No person shall harvest
marine plants except under the authority of a licence issued for that
purpose by the Minister under section 45 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 5
:::

### [Application for Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891831 .Subheading}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a document is to be made to the Minister on a form
  supplied by the Minister and accompanied by the appropriate fee.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2.1) to (2.3), the fee for a
  document set out in column I of an item of Part I of Schedule II is
  the fee set out in column II of that item.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2.2) and (2.3), if a
  commercial fishing licence authorizes the taking of specific
  quantities of any species set out in Part II of Schedule II, the fee
  for the licence shall be the sum of the products determined by
  multiplying the number of tonnes of each species set out in column I
  of Part II that are authorized to be taken under the licence from the
  waters set out in column II by the fee per tonne set out in column
  III.

- [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} Where any product calculated pursuant to subsection
  (2.1) in respect of any species of fish, other than groundfish,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is less than \$2,500.00 that product shall be
    reduced by 40 percent; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is \$2,500.00 or more that product shall be reduced
    by \$1,000.00.

- [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} Where the sum of the products calculated pursuant
  to subsection (2.1) in respect of all species of groundfish

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is less than \$2,500.00 that sum shall be reduced
    by 40 percent; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is \$2,500.00 or more that sum shall be reduced by
    \$1,000.00.

- [(3) to (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/93-61, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-1000, s. 2
- SOR/87-672, s. 2
- SOR/89-584, s. 2

- SOR/96-1, s. 1
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 2(E)](#1375823-1376726){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
:::

### [Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891845 .Subheading}

- **[[17.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to section 17, the
  Minister receives an application for a fisher's registration card or a
  vessel registration card, he may issue that card.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection (1), the Minister
  issues a fisher's registration card to a person or a vessel
  registration card in respect of a vessel, that person or vessel is
  registered with the Department.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 54(E)
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Inshore and Coastal Licences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1275296 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1290166 .Subheading}

- **[[17.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Part.

  [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      paragraph (a) of the definition [[related
      persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 251(2) of the
      [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([membre de la
      famille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore family fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore family fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that operates an inshore fishing enterprise and that
      meets the following conditions:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its voting shares are held by a licence
        holder;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sole director is the licence holder or, if
        the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of which
        belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its non-voting shares, if any, are held
        by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the licence holder's family or,
          if the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of
          which belong to one individual, a member of that individual's
          family, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing corporation or an inshore
          fishing family trust. ([société familiale de pêche
          côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that meets the following conditions:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its voting shares are held by a licence
        holder;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sole director is the licence holder or, if
        the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of which
        belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its non-voting shares, if any, are held
        by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the licence holder's family or,
          if the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of
          which belong to one individual, a member of that individual's
          family, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing corporation or an inshore
          fishing family trust. ([société de pêche
          côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore fishing family trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore fishing family trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trust

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that has as its sole trustee a licence holder
        or, if the licence holder is a corporation all of the shares of
        which belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which each beneficiary must be either a
        member of the licence holder's family or, if the licence holder
        is a corporation all of the shares of which belong to one
        individual, a member of that individual's family or an inshore
        fishing company. ([fiducie familiale de pêche
        côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[family
  member]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, common-law partnership is to be read
  as a person who is cohabiting with a licence holder in a relationship
  of a conjugal nature and has done so for a period of at least one
  year.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 4](#1275014-1290187){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275014-1290187.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Eligibility Criteria]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1275297 .Subheading}

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to the
following licences:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence that is held by a licence
  holder that is identified as an independent core licence holder with
  the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans Canada, except for a licence that
  indicates this Part does not apply, and the licence is to fish for

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} groundfish by means of fixed gear and the use of a
    vessel having an overall length of at least 13.72 m (45 feet) but
    not more than 19.81 m (65 feet),

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} groundfish by means of mobile gear and the use of
    a vessel having an overall length of less than 19.81 m (65 feet),

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} herring by means of a purse seine,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} scallops in respect of the Full Bay Fleet,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} shrimp by means of mobile gear in any of Shrimp
    Fishing Areas 13 to 15,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} swordfish by means of a longline,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} tuna, if it is a restricted licence and issued
    with a licence referred to in subparagraph (vi),

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} bluefin tuna, in respect of the southwest Nova
    Scotia fleet,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} sculpin, if the licence is issued to a licence
    holder that also holds a licence referred to in subparagraph (ii),

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} squid by means of jigger or handline and the
    licence is issued to a licence holder that also only holds a licence
    referred to in subparagraphs (i) to (ix),

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} squid by means of otter trawl and the licence is
    issued to a licence holder that also holds a licence referred to in
    subparagraph (i), (ii), (iv), (v), or (vii) to (ix) if an otter
    trawl is used as the means of fishing under that licence, or

  - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} squid by means of purse seine and the licence is
    issued to a licence holder that also holds a licence referred to in
    subparagraph (i) to (v), (vii), (viii) or (ix) if a purse seine is
    used as the means of fishing under that licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a coastal fishing licence, other than a licence to
  fish for herring or mackerel using fixed gear that is issued to a
  corporation with more than one shareholder;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence with "designated operator
  status" or "[exploitant désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an
  equivalent expression appearing on it and that is held by a licence
  holder referred to in paragraph (a);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence that is held by a licence
  holder that is identified as a head of a non-core enterprise with the
  Minister of Fisheries and Oceans Canada;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence with "designated operator
  status" or "[exploitant désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an
  equivalent expression appearing on it and that is held by a licence
  holder referred to in paragraph (d);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence held by an organization
  that has been issued an allocation of fish to catch for the benefit of
  its membership; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence held by a corporation that
  held such a licence before January 19, 1989, unless

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "designated operator status" or "[exploitant
    désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an equivalent expression appears
    on the licence, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the corporation held an inshore fishing licence
    before January 1, 1979.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 3](#1275012-1275321){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275012-1275321.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence referred to in paragraphs 18(a) to (f) may only be issued to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual or their estate or succession;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation, all of the shares of which belong to
    one individual; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an organization referred to in paragraph 18(f).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a licence referred to in paragraph
  18(a), (b), (d) or (g), the activities authorized under the licence
  must be carried out personally by the licence holder, the operator
  named in the licence or a person authorized in accordance with
  subsection 23(2) of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence referred to in paragraph 18(a) or (c) shall
  not be issued if, at the time of application, the applicant has
  transferred the use or control of the rights or privileges conferred
  under the licence for which the applicant is the licence holder or was
  the licence holder in the 12 months prior to the application or
  conferred under a licence that may be issued to the applicant.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A licence holder that has been denied the issuance of
  a licence under subsection (3) or has had a licence suspended or
  cancelled under paragraph 9(1)(b) of the Act and did not resolve the
  matter that gave rise to the denial, suspension or cancellation within
  12 months of the day on which the licence was denied, suspended or
  cancelled is never eligible to hold the same type of licence again.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in the following
  circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence is offered as security in a financial
    agreement under provincial legislation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph (a), a
    creditor that is a recognized financial institution uses or controls
    the rights or privileges under the licence in order to exercise its
    rights with respect to a security;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph (a), a
    creditor that is not a recognized financial institution, uses the
    privilege under the licence to recommend to the Minister the next
    licence holder or the reallocation of fishing quota, in order to
    exercise its rights with respect to a security;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a trustee or receiver appointed under the
    [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) uses or controls
    rights or privileges conferred by the licence;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the rights of the proceeds from the
    sale of the catch are transferred to any person on the vessel who
    participates in making the catch;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an applicant that is a licence
    holder, the rights and privileges under that licence or the rights
    and privileges to be granted if the application is accepted have
    been transferred to an inshore family fishing corporation of which
    the applicant holds 100% of the voting shares;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an applicant that is not a licence
    holder, the rights and privileges that are granted under the licence
    if the application is accepted have been transferred to a
    corporation that would be an inshore family fishing corporation of
    which the applicant holds 100% of the voting shares if the licence
    was issued;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} with the authorization of the Minister, the licence
    holder transfers the rights or privileges under the licence in order
    to give effect to the following arrangements made with other holders
    of the licences referred to in any of paragraphs 18(a) to (g):

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an arrangement for the reallocation of a fishing
      quota, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an arrangement for the reallocation of
      authorized fishing gear;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an organization referred to in paragraph 18(f) has
    required the licence holder to provide part of the proceeds from the
    sale of the catch back to the organization in exchange for
    additional fish allocation;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a liquidator of the succession, an executor or an
    administrator of the estate appointed upon the death of the licence
    holder uses or controls the rights or privileges under the licence;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a person is authorized to act on behalf of the
    licence holder in the case of their incapacity, including a
    guardian, committee, Public Guardian and Trustee, tutor, curator,
    Public Curator, mandatary under a protection mandate or any other
    person who is appointed to act in a similar capacity; or

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a person has received an authorization in
    accordance with subsection 23(2) of the [Fishery (General)
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) with respect to the
    licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 3](#1275012-1275329){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275012-1275329.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 5](#1275016-1290205){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275016-1290205.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20201123.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Requirements for Certain Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1290206 .Subheading}

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder referred
to in paragraph 18(a) or (c) shall keep a registry of the crew on board
the vessel on each fishing trip.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 6](#1275018-1290209){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275018-1290209.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Except in the cases
referred to in subsection 19(5), a holder of a licence referred to in
paragraph 18(a) or (c) shall not transfer the use or control of the
rights or privileges conferred under that licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 6](#1275018-1290212){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275018-1290212.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Except in the cases
referred to in subsection 19(5), no one other than the holder of a
licence referred to in paragraph 18(a) or (c) shall use or control the
rights and privileges conferred under that licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 6](#1275018-1290215){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275018-1290215.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[23 to 27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23_to_27 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/93-61, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

**[[28 to 32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28_to_32 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/91-296, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/93-61,
s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Spacing of Fishing Gear]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891855 .Part}

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided in the [Newfoundland and Labrador Fishery
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-78-443), the [Quebec Fishery
Regulations, 1990](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-214) or the [Maritime
Provinces Fishery Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-55) or as a
condition of a licence, no person shall fish with a drift net or any
fixed gear other than a hand-line within

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (b), 200 m of any previously set
  fixed gear other than a hand-line; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 300 m of a weir that is in fishing order.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, ss. 10, 53(F)
- SOR/2017-58, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 36"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  the master of a vessel fishing with mobile gear shall maintain a
  distance of at least one-half nautical mile between his vessel,
  including any mobile gear attached thereto, and any previously set
  fishing gear.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person is fishing for herring in Herring
  Fishing Area 15, 16, 20 or 21, he shall not operate mobile gear within
  one nautical mile of any previously set weir or trap net.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a person is fishing for capelin, herring or
  mackerel in any of the waters of Capelin Fishing Areas 1 to 14,
  Herring Fishing Areas 1 to 14 or Mackerel Fishing Areas 1 to 14, he
  shall not fish with mobile gear within 400 m of any previously set
  fixed gear.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 53(F)
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Pelagics]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891867 .Part}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2017-58,
s. 13\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Capelin]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891869 .Subheading}

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
capelin in a Capelin Fishing Area referred to in an item of Schedule V
with the type of vessel and gear set out in a subitem of that item
during the close time set out in that subitem.

### [Herring]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891871 .Subheading}

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 41 to
43, no person other than the holder of a bait fishing licence for
herring shall fish for herring in a Herring Fishing Area referred to in
an item of Schedule VII with the type of gear set out in a subitem of
that item during the close time set out in that subitem.

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
herring in the waters set out in an item of Schedule VIII with mobile
gear from a vessel of a length set out in that item during the close
time set out in that item.

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
herring in the waters of St. Paul's Inlet in Herring Fishing Area 14
lying inside of the Highway 430 bridge during the period beginning on
February 1 and ending on December 31.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 11(E)
:::

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** Where, pursuant to these
Regulations, a person is prohibited from fishing for herring at a time
and place at which he is engaged in a fishing trip for mackerel, that
person may retain a quantity of incidentally caught herring that does
not exceed a fishing quota of 10 per cent of the weight of the mackerel
caught and retained during the fishing trip.

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  no person shall fish for, buy, sell or have in his possession any
  herring that is less than 26.5 cm in length.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to herring
  that are less than 26.5 cm in length where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the catching of the herring was incidental to the
    catching of longer herring; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of herring less than 26.5 cm in length
    retained during any one fishing trip does not exceed 10% of the
    number of longer herring that were caught and retained during that
    fishing trip.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), the percentage
  shall be determined on the basis of four or more samples taken from
  the catch, with each sample containing 50 or more herring.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to herring
  caught under the authority of a licence for herring fishing with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gill nets;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any fishing gear in Herring Fishing Areas 20 or 21;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fixed gear in the St. Lawrence River estuary
    upstream of a straight line joining Latitude 48°54′N., Longitude
    68°40′W. to Latitude 48°38′N., Longitude 68°10′W.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 12(E)
- SOR/2003-137, s. 7(E)
:::

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, during
the period beginning on May 1 and ending on December 31, fish for
herring with a gill net that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size greater than 83 mm; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} contains monofilament in the netting.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 13
:::

**[[45.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2011-39, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-45.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 45.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[45.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45.2 .sectionLabel}** No person who is the
master of a fishing vessel shall land or permit to be landed from the
vessel, outside Canadian fisheries waters, herring caught by means of
mobile gear unless, before it is landed,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the herring is inspected by a fishery officer at a
  Canadian port; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where no fishery officer is available to inspect the
  herring at a Canadian port, the master of the vessel obtains
  permission from a fishery officer to land the herring outside Canadian
  fisheries waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-203, s. 2
:::

**[[45.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45.3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2011-39, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-45.3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 45.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Mackerel]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891902 .Subheading}

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 47, no
person other than the holder of a licence for bait fishing for mackerel
shall fish for mackerel in a Mackerel Fishing Area referred to in an
item of Schedule X with a type of vessel and gear set out in a subitem
of that item during the close time set out in that subitem.

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** Where, pursuant to these
Regulations, a person is prohibited from fishing for mackerel at a time
and place at which he is engaged in a fishing trip for herring, that
person may retain a quantity of incidentally caught mackerel that does
not exceed a fishing quota of 10 per cent of the weight of the herring
caught and retained during the fishing trip.

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  a person shall not fish for, buy, sell or have in their possession any
  mackerel that is less than 26.8 cm in length.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to
  mackerel that are less than 26.8 cm in length if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the catching of the mackerel was incidental to the
    catching of longer mackerel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of mackerel less than 26.8 cm in length
    retained during any one fishing trip does not exceed 10% of the
    number of longer mackerel caught and retained during the fishing
    trip.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), the percentage
  shall be determined on the basis of four or more samples taken from
  the catch, with each sample containing 50 or more mackerel.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply with respect to
  mackerel caught with gill nets.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 14(E)
- SOR/2003-137, s. 8(E)
- [SOR/2021-100, s. 2](#1292935-1293192){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1292935-1293192.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-48-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 48"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, during
the period beginning on May 1 and ending on December 31, fish for
mackerel with a gill net that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size greater than 83 mm; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} contains monofilament in the netting.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 15
:::

### [Recreational Mackerel Fishing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1293193 .Subheading}

**[[49.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.01 .sectionLabel}** A person engaged in
recreational fishing for mackerel in Mackerel Fishing Areas 1 to 21
shall not

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any one day, have in their possession more than 20
  mackerel;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} have in their possession any mackerel that is less
  than 26.8 cm in length;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fish for mackerel during the close time of January 1
  to March 31; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} fish for mackerel with more than five fishing lines
  or with a fishing line to which more than six hooks are attached.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-100, s. 3](#1292942-1293200){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1292942-1293200.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Shark]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891919 .Subheading}

**[[49.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for shark with any gear other than angling gear, a hand-line or longline
gear.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-292, s. 3
- SOR/2005-268, s. 2
:::

- **[[49.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, for commercial purposes, fish for a species of shark set
  out in column I of an item of the table to this subsection in the
  waters set out in column II from a vessel of a vessel class set out in
  column III during the close time set out in column IV of the item.

### [Swordfish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891951 .Subheading}

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for swordfish in the waters set out in column I of
  the item of the table to this subsection from a vessel of a vessel
  class set out in column II during the close time set out in column III
  of the item.

  <figure id="891954">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The close time set out in column III of the table
  to subsection (1) may be varied under subsection 6(1) of the [Fishery
  (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) with respect to any
  of the vessel classes referred to in column II.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for swordfish in the waters
  set out in column I of the item of the table to this subsection with a
  type of gear set out in a subitem in column II during the close time
  set out in that subitem in column III of the item.

  <figure id="891962">

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person shall be in
  possession of any swordfish that is less than 125 cm in length.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the catching of the swordfish that are less than
    125 cm in length was incidental to the catching of longer swordfish;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of swordfish less than 125 cm in length
    that are retained during any one fishing trip does not exceed a
    fishing quota of 15 per cent of the number of longer swordfish that
    are retained during that fishing trip.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-292, s. 4
- SOR/2005-268, s.3
- SOR/2008-99, s. 2
:::

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Shellfish]{.HTitleText1} {#h-891974 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-61, s. 16(F)

\]
:::

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[Atlantic razor clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Atlantic razor clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the
    scientific name of which is [Ensis directus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([couteau de l'Atlantique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Atlantic razor clam, the
    soft-shell clam and the surf clam; ([clam]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[drag rake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drag rake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device used in fishing for
    shellfish that is towed along the bed of the water by a vessel, but
    does not include a hydraulic device or a mechanical device; ([râteau
    traînant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hydraulic device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hydraulic device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device used in
    fishing for shellfish whereby the shellfish are lifted from the bed
    of the water by the use of water under pressure; ([dispositif
    hydraulique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2017-58, s.
    14\]]{.Repealed}

[[mechanical device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mechanical device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any device or
    equipment used in fishing for shellfish that is not

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a hand-held tool,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a drag rake, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a hydraulic device; ([dispositif
      mécanique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mussel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mussel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the scientific
    name of which is [Mytilus edulis, Mytilus trossulus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} or [Modiolus modiolus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([moule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[oyster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[oyster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the scientific
    name of which is [Crassostrea virginica]{.otherLang lang="la"} or
    [Ostrea edulis]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([huître]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[shell height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shell height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2017-58, s.
    14\]]{.Repealed}

[[soft-shell clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[soft-shell clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the
    scientific name of which is [Mya arenaria]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([mye]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Stimpson's surf clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Stimpson's surf clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the
    scientific name of which is [Mactromeris polynyma]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([mactre de Stimpson]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[surf clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[surf clam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the shellfish the scientific
    name of which is [Spisula solidissima]{.otherLang lang="la"}, but
    does not include the Stimpson's surf
    clam. ([mactre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2017-58, s.
    14\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-672, s. 3
- SOR/93-61, s. 17
- SOR/2003-314, s. 2
- SOR/2017-58, s. 14
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 3](#1375825-1376728){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375825-1376728.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Clams, Mussels and Oysters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-891996 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-891997 .Subheading}

- **[[51.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 51.2 to 51.9 apply in
  respect of fishing for clams, mussels and oysters in the inland waters
  and tidal waters, including the foreshore of those waters, within or
  adjacent to the Province of Quebec.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 13 does not apply in respect of fishing for
  clams, mussels and oysters in the waters referred to in subsection
  (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 18
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

#### [Length Restrictions, Close Times and Other Restrictions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-892003 .Subheading}

**[[51.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch
and retain or possess any shellfish of a species set out in column I of
an item of the table to this section, the length of which is less than
the minimum length set out in column II of the item.

<figure id="892005">

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

- **[[51.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in commercial fishing for a species set out in
  column II of an item of Schedule X.1 in the waters set out in column I
  by a method set out in column III during the close time set out in
  column IV of the item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in recreational fishing for a
  species set out in column II of an item of Schedule X.1 in the waters
  set out in column I during the close time set out in column IV of the
  item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

#### [Licences]{.HTitleText3} {#h-892018 .Subheading}

- **[[51.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall fish for clams, mussels or oysters unless the person is
  authorized to do so under a licence issued under these Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to persons engaged in
  recreational fishing for clams, mussels or oysters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

**[[51.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.5 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on
application and payment of the fee set out in column II of an item of
the table to this section, issue a licence described in column I of the
item.

<figure id="892025">

class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize9 topdouble topbot">

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

#### [Fishing for Purposes of Replanting]{.HTitleText3} {#h-892033 .Subheading}

**[[51.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.6 .sectionLabel}** Despite any length
restriction or close time set out in this Part, a person may, under the
authority of a fishing licence for the purpose of replanting issued
under this Part, fish for clams, mussels or oysters for the purpose of
replanting them in a location that supports their growth, their
condition or their accessibility.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

#### [Recreational Fishing]{.HTitleText3} {#h-892037 .Subheading}

**[[51.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.7 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for clams, mussels or oysters shall fish by any
means other than by means of hand or hand-held tools.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

**[[51.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.8 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for clams, mussels or oysters shall fish during the
period that begins half an hour after sunset on one day and ends half an
hour before sunrise on the following day.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

**[[51.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.9 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for clams, mussels or oysters shall catch and
retain, in any one day, a quantity of a species of shellfish set out in
column I of an item of the table to this section that is greater than
the quota set out in column II of the item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-314, s. 3
:::

### [Crab]{.HTitleText2} {#h-892053 .Subheading}

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, in a
Crab Fishing Area set out in column I of an item of Schedule XII, during
the close time set out in column II of that item,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 55, fish for crab;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} possess any crab unless the crab is being transported
  under the authority of a fish transporting licence issued under these
  Regulations; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 52.1, have a crab trap on board a
  vessel.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 19
:::

- **[[52.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fishery officer may in writing authorize a person to transport crab
  traps during a close time in a Crab Fishing Area for the purpose of
  the sale, repair or storage of those traps.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not transport crab traps pursuant to
  an authorization referred to in subsection (1) except from and to such
  locations, in such quantities and on such dates as are set out in that
  authorization.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 19
:::

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** No person shall have in
his possession

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any female crab;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any snow crab that is less than 95 mm in width; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a Crab Fishing Area, any crab part or crab meat
  separated from the carapace of a crab.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-232, s. 1
:::

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 55, no person
  shall fish for crab except from a vessel and with a crab trap.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish with or have on board a vessel

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a crab trap that has a mesh size less than 65 mm;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a crab trap the volume of which exceeds 2.1 m^3^
    when measured on the exterior.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In Crab Fishing Areas 1 to 11, no person shall fish
  with or have on board a vessel

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a crab trap other than a cone-shaped trap; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cone-shaped crab trap that has a bottom ring with
    an inside diameter greater than 133 cm.

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** Where a person is fishing
for lobster in accordance with these Regulations, that person may,
without a licence for crab fishing, retain any male rock crabs caught in
his lobster traps.

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2013-37,
s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Lobster]{.HTitleText2} {#h-892081 .Subheading}

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, in a Lobster Fishing Area set out in column I of an item
  of Schedule XIV, during the close time set out in column II of that
  item,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish for lobster;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} possess any lobster unless the lobster is being
    transported under the authority of a fish transporting licence
    issued under these Regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 58, have a lobster trap on board
    a vessel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess, in a Lobster Fishing Area
  set out in column I of an item of Schedule XIV, a lobster of a length
  that is less than the length set out in column III of that item.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess, in a Lobster Fishing Area
  set out in column I of an item of Schedule XIV, any claw, tail or meat
  that has been separated from the thorax or carapace of a lobster.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for lobster in the lagoons of
  the Magdalen Islands in Lobster Fishing Area 22.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 21
- SOR/93-215, s. 1
:::

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fishery officer may in writing authorize a person to transport lobster
  traps during a close time in a Lobster Fishing Area for the purpose of
  the sale, repair or storage of those traps.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not transport lobster traps pursuant
  to an authorization referred to in subsection (1) except from and to
  such locations, in such quantities and on such dates as are set out in
  that authorization.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 22
:::

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), it is
  prohibited for any person to buy, sell or have in their possession any
  lobster that is less than 72 mm in length.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In Newfoundland and Labrador, it is prohibited for
  any person to buy, sell or have in their possession any lobster that
  is less than 82.5 mm in length.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall buy, sell or have in their possession
  any female lobster with eggs attached.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall buy, sell or have in his possession

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any female lobster from which eggs have been washed
    or removed in whole or in part; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any female lobster that has egg cement or glue on
    its swimmerets.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-61, s. 23(F)
- SOR/2003-137, s. 9
- SOR/2017-58, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for lobster except from a vessel and with a lobster
  trap.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), where a person is fishing
  for lobster, he shall not have any mobile gear on board his vessel.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence for bait fishing that
  authorizes the use of an otter trawl in fishing for bait may have an
  otter trawl on board his vessel while he is fishing for lobster in
  Lobster Fishing Area 22.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except in Lobster Fishing Area 41, no person shall
  fish with or have on board a vessel a lobster trap that exceeds

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 125 cm in its greatest length;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 90 cm in its greatest width; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 50 cm in its greatest height.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/93-30, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/96-220, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-440, s. 1
- SOR/89-85, s. 1(F)
- SOR/89-204, s. 1
- SOR/93-30, s. 1
- SOR/96-220, s. 1
:::

**[[61.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61.1 .sectionLabel}** In Lobster Fishing
Areas 1 to 22 and 27 to 41, no person shall fish with, or have on board
a vessel, a lobster trap unless the trap

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has in one exterior wall of each parlour an escape
  panel that provides, when removed, an unobstructed opening not less
  than 89 mm in height and 152 mm in width and that is fastened to the
  lobster trap with

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} untreated cotton or sisal twine that does not
    exceed 4.8 mm in diameter, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} uncoated ferrous metal wire, other than of
    stainless steel, that does not exceed 1.6 mm in diameter; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a wooden lobster trap that has in one exterior
  wall of each parlour two softwood laths that are adjacent to each
  other and that are not treated with a wood preservative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-30, s. 2
:::

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2013-37,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

Version](section-62-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}

### [Scallops]{.HTitleText2} {#h-892127 .Subheading}

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  for the waters described in subsections (3) to (5), no person shall
  fish for scallops in a Scallop Fishing Area referred to in column I of
  an item of Schedule XVI from a vessel of a class set out in column II
  of that item during the close time set out opposite that class of
  vessel in column III thereof.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/87-672, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for scallops by any means during
  the period May 1 to November 30 in the waters of Digby Gut and
  Annapolis Basin in Scallop Fishing Area 28A inside of a straight line
  joining Latitude 44°41′30″N., Longitude 65°47′12″W. (Light Tower at
  Prim Point) to Latitude 44°41′12″N., Longitude 65°45′36″W. (Fog Alarm
  Building).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for scallops by any means during
  the period of May 1 to September 30 in that portion of Scallop Fishing
  Area 28A that is within six nautical miles of the shores of Annapolis
  and Digby Counties and lying between a line bearing 342° true from
  Latitude 44°48′52″N., Longitude 65°32′21″W. (East Breakwater, Parkers
  Cove, Annapolis County, Nova Scotia) and a line bearing 342° true from
  Latitude 44°29′56″N., Longitude 66°06′30″W. (Sandy Cove, Digby County,
  Nova Scotia) but not including the waters of Digby Gut and Annapolis
  Basin described in subsection (3).

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for scallops by means of a
  scallop drag during the period beginning on April 1 and ending at
  08:00 on the second Tuesday in January next following in that portion
  of Scallop Fishing Area 28B (the waters in the area of Grand Manan
  Island) bounded by straight lines joining the following points in the
  order in which they are listed:

- [(4.2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for scallops by any means
  during the period beginning on April 1 and ending at 08:00 on the

  Fishing Area 28B lying in the Bay of Fundy bounded by the
  Canada/United States Boundary; a straight line joining a point on the

  shall fish for scallops by any means in that portion of Scallop
  Fishing Area 29 bounded by the coastline of Lunenburg County, Nova

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), no person
  shall catch and retain or have on board a vessel any scallops the
  number of meats of which exceeds an average count of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 72 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were caught
    in Scallop Fishing Area 28A, 28B, 28C or 28D during the period May 1
    to September 30;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 55 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were caught
    in Scallop Fishing Area 28A, 28B, 28C or 28D during the period
    October 1 to April 30;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 52 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were caught
    in Scallop Fishing Area 22 or 24;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} 39 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were
    caught in Scallop Fishing Area 21;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 45 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were caught
    in Scallop Fishing Area 25; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 33 meats per 500 g, where the scallops were caught
    in Scallop Fishing Area 1, 2, 11 to 20, 23, 26, 27 or 29 or in that
    part of Scallop Fishing Area 10 west of Longitude 55°10′W.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the average count
  shall be determined on the basis of eight or more samples of scallop
  meats, each sample weighing 500 g or more.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), any scallops
  caught and retained or any scallops or scallop meats on board a vessel
  shall be regarded as having been caught in the Scallop Fishing Area in
  which that vessel is permitted to fish under a licence or under a
  written authorization referred to in subsection 66(1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person fishing for
  scallops

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} under a licence for recreational fishing; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from a vessel less than 19.8 m in overall length in
    an area other than Scallop Fishing Area 21, 22, 23, 24, 27, 28A,
    28B, 28C, 28D or 29.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2017-58, s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} catch and retain or have on board a vessel in
    Scallop Fishing Area 28A, 28B, 28C or 28D any scallop unless the
    shell height of that scallop is 76 mm or greater.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-672, s. 5
- SOR/88-208, s. 1
- SOR/91-225, s. 1
- SOR/94-59, s. 2
- SOR/2008-99, s. 3
- SOR/2017-58, s. 16
:::
